金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 23 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國擴大 AI 晶片管制範圍，全球算力供應鏈面臨重組：美國 BIS 於 11/16 公布新規，將先進
晶片管制由實體出口延伸至第三國資料中心提供的遠端算力服務；向中國等受限地區提供合計
逾 1,200 萬 TPP (總處理效能) 算力的業者，出口特定晶片及設備須取得許可，並新增對東南
亞及巴西等地的出口許可要求。新規可能提高輝達、超微及海外資料中心合規成本，美系大型
雲端業者相對受惠，後續關注訂單轉移及全球 AI 算力部署變化。

◼ 輝達財報維持高速成長，SEC 會計調查疑慮重創市場信心：輝達 11/17 公布季度營收達 1,158
億美元，年增 103%，其中資料中心營收年增 110%至 1,076 億美元，顯示 AI 運算需求仍具
韌性。然而，11/20 Reuters 報導 SEC 正在檢視輝達可能涉及的會計問題，輝達當日股價重挫
14%，全週下跌約 23%。市場疑慮由 AI 投資回報延伸至財務透明度及企業治理，加劇半導體
產業賣壓，惟具體會計違規事項尚未獲確認。
◼ Fed 會議紀要釋出偏鷹訊號，市場重新評估升息風險：Fed 於 10 月 FOMC 會議中，以 10 比
2 票決議維持政策利率於 3.75%至 4.00%，兩名反對委員主張升息 1 碼；9 月整體及核心 PCE
通膨年增率估達 3.6%及 3.1%，顯示關稅、能源與 AI 投資需求仍帶來物價壓力。經濟與就業
雖具韌性，但通膨朝 2%目標回落的進展仍不穩定，市場須留意利率維持高檔甚至再升息的可
能，美債殖利率及高評價股票走勢將更受通膨與 Fed 政策影響。

市場展望
◼ 短線審慎，中期審慎偏正向，關注企業獲利與資金面變化：全球科技股修正已由評價調整擴及
企業治理、政策及資金面，市場風險偏好明顯下降，短期賣壓仍待消化。惟 AI 運算需求尚未
明顯反轉，主要 CSP 持續投入 AI 基礎建設，中長期成長動能仍具支撐。因此，美國科技股及
具 AI 供應鏈優勢的台、日市場，短期維持審慎，中期審慎偏正向。後續應聚焦企業獲利、訂
單及現金流，並關注 CSP 資本支出、Fed 政策與信用風險，配置以技術領先、財務穩健企業
為主。
◼ 市場避險情緒上升，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：Fed 政策與通膨不確定性仍可能造成利率
波動，但股市風險偏好下降推升美債避險需求，當前殖利率亦具配置吸引力。考量殖利率水準、
利率敏感度及信用品質，中天期美國公債與投資等級公司債風險報酬相對均衡，展望偏正向。
超長天期公債仍須留意財政赤字及期限溢價風險，投資級債整體信用品質穩健，惟須持續關注
AI 融資疑慮及信用利差擴大風險。

科技動能支撐股市，利率風險牽動債市配置
2026 年 9 月
全球股市 8 月截至 26 日多數上漲，其中成熟市場上漲 3.1%居冠，新興亞洲、東協股市與標普
500 也分別上漲 2.9%、2.7%及 2.6%。美股受企業財報韌性與月初就業、通膨數據降溫支撐；亞洲
則受美元偏弱、油價與長端殖利率回落帶動風險偏好改善。然而，中國股市期間下跌 0.3%，該國 7
月零售、工業生產與投資數據偏弱，加上阿里巴巴折價配股一度衝擊科技股，惟公司高層後續增持有
助緩解信心壓力。拉美股市下跌 0.8%，在主要股市中敬陪末座，主要拖累來自巴西；8 月截至 24 日，

外資淨流出巴西股市約 214 億雷亞爾，加上財政與 10 月大選的不確定性壓抑市場。印度亦小跌
0.3%，因高油價與 RBI 偏鷹訊號使公債殖利率上升。
全球債市 8 月截至 26 日則全面收紅，以新興當地債上漲 1.53%居冠，美元指數回落至 99 附近，
為其帶來匯兌加成；亞洲高收益債與全球高收益債分別上漲 1.16%、1.09%，高票息與較短存續期提
供支撐。亞洲債券、全球主權債與新興美元債表現相對落後。8 月中美國 30 年期殖利率一度升至 2007
年以來高點，壓抑長存續期債券；其後油價回落，加上 8/19 財政部宣布 9/9 起將 10 至 30 年期公債
買回上限至少倍增至每次 40 億美元，長端流動性疑慮獲緩解，30 年期殖利率回落，因而帶動債券價
格修復。
展望 9 月，市場焦點將集中於 9 月 15 日至 16 日的 FOMC 會議，以及最新經濟預測與點陣圖；
全球股市預料呈現高檔震盪格局。儘管物價漲勢稍有緩和，但 Fed 對通膨仍保持警戒；若政策立場
較市場預期鷹派，可能推升美債殖利率波動，並壓抑高估值成長股走勢。不過，AI 基礎建設與企業

科技投資趨勢延續，美國企業獲利成長亦有利科技股表現，預料中期基本面仍具支撐。台灣受 AI 與
半導體出口需求帶動，經濟成長動能強勁；日股則持續受企業治理改革與資本效率改善支撐。整體而
言，9 月股市短線震盪難免，但中期仍看好科技股，以及基本面相對強勁的台灣與日本股市。
就債市而言，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔；若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利
率上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短的存續期間亦伴隨再投資
風險；10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。相較之下，3 至 10 年期債券兼具較高息收、
適度存續期間與參與利率下行的空間。整體而言，目前 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬
條件相對均衡，較具表現機會。

美國股市：AI 熱潮推升企業獲利，美股續強但留意利率風險
八月美股在高檔震盪中收高，科技股表現亮眼，除整體美股優異財報支撐指數表現外，AI 龍頭企
業如 NVIDIA 均給出強勁展望，AI 行情由晶片硬體向軟體與資安應用端擴散。指數上揚主要由輝達、
微軟、亞馬遜、帕蘭提爾、特斯拉、Meta、美光科技、賽富時、Marvell、默克藥廠等企業帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括蘋果、博通、沃爾瑪、TJX、AppLovin、威騰、聯合健康、CVS、應用材料、
超微半導體等。
聯準會新任主席試圖以新的溝通模式取代傳統前瞻指引，市場解讀為基準利率有上行空間且更長

時間維持高檔。聯準會主席華許在全球央行年會(Jackson Hole)拒絕提供傳統的前瞻指引或機械式的
反應函數，強調央行過度承諾未來利率路徑會失去政策彈性，以一套「決策準則」取代指引，重點觀
察潛在通膨是否快速回到 2%、勞動市場是否偏離充分就業，以及金融條件是否有效限制總需求。由
於 7 月 FOMC 會議出現官員主張升息的情況，使短端與長端美債殖利率走勢分化，殖利率曲線進一
步陡峭化，市場正在質疑 Fed 是否具備一致且可理解的決策框架，但在華許發言後，按照這套標準，
目前政策訊號仍偏向利率在較高水準維持更長時間。由於通膨問題仍具廣度，同時信用利差偏低、股
市高檔、AI 投資與私人需求持續強勁，很難將目前金融條件視為具有限制性，故市場預期聯準會可
能不只提高升息機率，也提高了升息後維持高利率的可能性，甚至進一步提高未來降息的門檻。
第二季財報公布大致落幕，表現優於先前預期。目前標普 500 成分股已有 459 家公布 2Q26 財
報，其中 86%獲利優於市場預期，並帶動 2Q26 獲利年增達約 31.6%，較季初市場預估的 21%大幅

上修；全年獲利年增亦由 23%上修至 27%。類股獲利年增動能以能源(YoY+145%)、資訊科技
(YoY+71%)、原材料(YoY+41%)增長動能最強，醫療保健類股(YoY-7%)表現落後。資訊科技類股獲
利成長主要由半導體族群帶動，次產業中半導體獲利年增 139%、科技硬體與設備獲利年增 50.9%。
美國期中選舉前的「資料中心用電排擠效應引發的民粹反彈」持續發酵。隨著 AI 資料中心龐大
的電力需求，引發地方民意對民生與傳統產業用電資源遭擠壓的疑慮持續升溫。各州政府在選舉壓力
下，部分地方政府緊急凍結資料中心計畫，預料 11 月期中選舉後政治干擾將可降溫。
目前標普 500 指數位於歷史高檔位置，本益比已升至 23.51 倍、股價淨值比達 5.6 倍，短期評價
偏高。雖然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但股市漲幅已高，短期仍需留意市場過熱與估
值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：景氣溫和復甦，歐洲央行升息預期與政治變數限制評價 
八月歐股延續多頭走勢，泛歐 600 指數中原物料與科技股表現相對強勢，包括思愛普、艾司摩爾
控股、Argenx、施耐德電機、力拓、嘉能可、英美資源、安聯集團、勞斯萊斯控股、西門子等表現
較佳；相較之下，部分金融與消費股表現偏弱，包括雀巢、匯豐控股、諾和諾德、英美菸草、阿斯特
捷利康、法國巴黎銀行、法國興業銀行、諾華、百威英博、萬喜等。 
歐元區八月綜合 PMI 升至 52.1，連續第 2 個月站上榮枯線，顯示第 3 季初景氣延續擴張。其中
製造業升至 52.8，創 2022 年 5 月以來最佳表現，復甦已由生產端擴散至需求端。AI 相關科技與設
備需求增加，加上國防支出擴大，帶動製造業需求回升；服務業則持平於 51.7，受惠於旅遊維持溫
和擴張。國家別來看，德國製造業 PMI 升至 54.1，創 2022 年以來最佳表現，法國製造業重返擴張
區間，但極端高溫拖累服務業活動。整體而言，八月 PMI 顯示歐元區景氣動能明顯改善，製造業加
速復甦，短期衰退風險進一步下降，然而服務業擴張力道未見加快，復甦步調呈現分化。 
部分歐洲央行官員表示，九月已有充分理由升息，利率交換市場目前反映 ECB 在 9 月會議升息
一碼的可能性。美伊戰爭推高能源成本，加上歐洲經濟表現穩健，使市場認為 ECB 仍有收緊貨幣政
策的空間。市場焦點因此由「ECB 會不會升息」轉向「升息循環還會延續多久」。中東局勢仍是最大
變數，若能源成本持續走高且逐步傳導至商品、服務價格與薪資，ECB 第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場。 
9 月歐股須留意政治局勢。若 AfD（德國另類選擇黨）於地方選舉取得大勝，雖不會直接改變目
前政府執政地位，但可能削弱改革推進速度，造成市場風險溢價上升；另外英國新任首相柏南承諾維
持既有財政規則，並受到工黨不提高所得稅、國民保險及主要稅率承諾的約束，使政府在國防、公共
服務與生活成本支出增加之際，財政操作空間相當有限，市場因此高度關注政府是否轉向其他形式的
增稅。若改革措施過度激進推高英國公債殖利率，恐將造成市場震盪。 
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦，但原油價格再次走高是最大隱憂。若能源成本居高不下，歐洲央行
與英國央行第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場，進而導致產業表現分化。銀行與金融類股可望受惠於利率走
高及淨利差改善；反之，科技、醫療保健及其他成長型類股將面臨殖利率上升所帶來的評價壓力。目
前泛歐 600 指數本益比約 16.5 倍，股價淨值比約 2.5 倍。雖然俄烏戰爭與通膨再度升溫風險仍需關
注，但在評價合理的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

資料來源：彭博,2026/8/31

日本股市：通膨回溫拉高升息預期，半導體與內需動能推升經濟表現
八月日股受地緣政治風險與殖利率走高影響，日經 225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，強勢族群包括瑞
可利控股、愛德萬測試、藤倉、軟銀、松下控股、村田製作所、瑞穗金融、東京威力科創、三菱 UFJ
金融、揖斐電等；弱勢股包括東京海上控股、迅銷、大金工業、富士軟片、日本電裝、發那科、7&I、
丸紅、損保控股、KDDI 等。
8 月東京核心 CPI 升至 1.8%，雖能源補貼與食品價格降溫壓抑整體物價，但租金與醫療服務價
格明顯上升；扣除食品與能源後通膨加速至 1.4%，顯示內生性通膨壓力仍在。若就十大產品分項來

看，引導本月通膨的上漲主要來自房屋及醫療保健，其中租金價格年增幅達 1.9%，反映物價上漲持
續傳遞至房市；醫療保健價格年增幅亦自前月的 1.4%攀升至 2.8%，因政府將高額療養費制度內的自
付費用上限調高所致。根據總務省估算，日本政府對能源補貼的政策約壓抑本月通膨 0.27 個百分點，
顯示若扣除政策影響，則當前通膨約為 2.2%，仍高於日本央行所設定的物價目標。雖政府補貼政策
將持續對通膨帶來壓抑效果，使得整體通膨略低於 2%，然而搭配央行對日圓匯率過於弱勢、恐進一
步墊高通膨的擔憂，預估將促使日本央行最快於 9 月份升息一碼。
日本半導體製造裝置協會（SEAJ）公布 7 月日本製晶片設備銷售額（3 個月移動平均值）為 5,562.2
億日圓，月銷售額年增 35.4%，創下 2024 年 12 月以來最大增幅。同時，若觀察今年 1 至 7 月份累
計銷售額達 3.44 兆日圓，年增 15.7%，顯示隨著當前人工智慧墊高晶片需求，推升廠商對半導體設
備的需求。
日本 7 月外國旅客入境人數為 344.2 萬人次，年增 0.1%。本月份外國旅客人數最大宗仍是來自

南韓，達 89.5 萬人，年增 31.9%。其次則為台灣遊客的 76.4 萬人次，年增 26.4%。與之相反，來
自中國的遊客人數年減幅達 56.1%，因日中兩國關係遲遲未見改善所致。根據日本百貨店協會公布的
資料顯示，7 月百貨公司免稅銷售額年增 25.5%至 506 億日圓，連續五個月的正成長，顯示在日圓
延續弱勢的格局之下，外國旅客消費力仍有提升空間。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、日圓貶值、高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政
策利多支撐，雖然日圓在政府干預與 BOJ 升息下繼續貶值空間受限，出口企業的匯兌紅利將逐步消
退，但預料日圓接下來仍以區間整理機會較高，並無大幅升值空間。目前 TOPIX 指數本益比約 17.7
倍、股價淨值比約 1.9 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：工業生產創高、景氣續亮紅燈，AI 供應鏈撐起製造業擴張動能
8 月台股在 AI 龍頭財報優於預期及全球 AI 資本支出持續擴張帶動下，市場氣氛維持偏多，但受
到 Jackson Hole 會議後市場重新調整聯準會政策預期、美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強影響，指數於高
檔呈現震盪整理。資金面上，高評價 AI 族群漲多後出現輪動，市場開始重新檢視企業未來獲利成長
能否持續支撐評價水準。不過整體而言，AI 資料中心、半導體、先進封裝及伺服器供應鏈需求仍維
持強勁，有助於消化部分評價壓力，並使市場焦點重新回到企業基本面與獲利成長。

當前臺灣經濟仍處於景氣擴張階段。全球 AI 建置需求推動企業設備投資與科技資本支出維持高
檔，相關需求正逐步由晶片擴散至資料中心、網通設備、電力基礎建設及相關零組件。從國際環境觀
察，美國企業投資及私人需求維持韌性，AI 相關支出已成為支撐美國與全球科技景氣的重要力量，
也持續為台灣出口與半導體供應鏈提供結構性成長動能。
展望 9 月，市場關注焦點將由企業財報逐漸轉向總體經濟數據與聯準會政策方向。Jackson Hole
會議後，市場對於聯準會維持較高利率期間的預期升溫，短期可能提高全球金融市場波動。然而，從
企業基本面觀察，NVIDIA 最新展望顯示 AI 需求仍具高度能見度，全球雲端業者與大型企業的 AI 基
礎建設支出並未出現明顯放緩跡象。在此背景下，只要半導體、伺服器及先進封裝產業維持強勁接單
動能，台灣電子產業景氣仍有機會延續高檔表現。
投資策略方面，仍可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但須更重視獲利成長與評價合理性。除了先
進製程及先進封裝外，AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、電源管理、高速傳輸、網通設備及記憶體等受惠 AI 基

建擴張之族群，仍具備中長期成長機會。隨著 AI 投資逐步由 GPU 擴散至記憶體、網路交換器、光通
訊及系統整合等領域，受惠範圍亦由少數龍頭供應商進一步擴展至整體供應鏈，有助於支撐相關企業
未來營收與獲利成長動能。另一方面，9 月需留意美國 CPI 數據、聯準會利率決策及美債殖利率變化
對高評價科技股的影響；若市場因政策預期、資金輪動或短期獲利了結而出現回檔，較可能屬於多頭
趨勢中的震盪整理，而非基本面反轉。在企業獲利持續成長、AI 資本支出維持高檔及全球科技景氣
延續復甦的背景下，建議布局方向可由單純題材面轉向具備技術門檻、訂單能見度及現金流品質較佳
的個股，以提升投資組合在景氣循環與市場波動中的穩健度。

中國股市：硬科技 IPO 熱潮延燒，A 股中報揭露「增收不增利」普遍現象
8 月中國經濟延續「總量承壓、科技產業相對強勢」的分化格局。儘管房地產調整與內需復甦力
道仍有限，但高端製造、人工智慧及數位經濟仍是政策資源與市場資金聚焦方向。在傳統產業景氣復
甦較為緩慢的情況下，科技產業逐漸成為支撐經濟成長的重要動能，顯示中國經濟正持續朝新舊動能
轉換與產業升級方向發展。
科技產業方面，人形機器人已成為近期最受市場關注的新興產業之一。根據中國國家市場監管總
局統計，2026 年上半年人形機器人領域新設企業數量達 11.6 萬家，較 2025 年同期的 7.2 萬家大幅

成長 61.1%，反映資本投入、技術研發與產業參與程度快速提升。隨著人工智慧、大模型及感測技術
進步，人形機器人正逐步由研發驗證邁向商業化應用階段，產業發展重點也由概念題材轉向量產能
力、產品可靠度及終端需求驗證。
資本市場表現雖顯示科技板塊仍是 A 股資金主要聚焦方向，但企業基本面改善幅度與市場期待之
間仍存在落差。從滬深 300 成分股來看，科技類企業近年營收維持增長，但獲利表現並未同步大幅
提升，反映部分企業仍面臨研發投入增加、產能擴張及市場競爭加劇等壓力。AI、半導體及高端製造
雖持續獲得政策支持與資本關注，但不少企業的估值已提前反映未來成長預期，實際獲利貢獻仍有待
進一步驗證。市場關注焦點也逐漸由政策題材與自主替代概念，轉向訂單能見度、現金流與獲利能力
等基本面指標；若後續營運表現未能跟上市場預期，不排除科技板塊仍面臨評價修正壓力。
展望 9 月，政策方向預計仍以穩增長與培育新質生產力為主軸，人工智慧、人形機器人、高端製
造及半導體等領域仍將是支持重點。在整體經濟復甦力道尚未全面擴散的背景下，市場表現預期仍將

呈現結構性分化，資金持續聚焦具成長潛力與技術門檻的科技產業。投資策略上，可留意人形機器人
產業鏈、工業自動化、AI 應用、半導體設備及高端製造等受惠產業升級趨勢之族群；惟部分熱門題
材評價已反映較高成長預期，後續仍須觀察訂單落地、量產進度及獲利改善情況，避免因短期市場情
緒過熱而提高投資風險。

科技類股：AI 基礎建設持續擴張，算力、光通訊與平台生態同步受惠
全球科技產業的核心成長動能仍來自 AI 基礎建設擴張，但市場焦點已逐步由單純 GPU 採購轉向
整體資料中心架構升級。隨 Google 擴大投入光通訊與高速網路技術，AI 資料中心正朝向更高頻寬、
更低延遲及更高能效方向發展。市場預估 800G 至 1.6T 光通訊滲透率將持續提升，推動光模組、CPO、
交換器及相關供應鏈需求成長，也反映大型雲端服務供應商仍持續強化 AI 基礎建設布局。在此趨勢
下，AI 投資已由單一晶片擴展至網路、儲存、記憶體及先進封裝等完整生態系，帶動相關供應鏈重

新評價。
企業獲利方面，NVIDIA 最新財報再次驗證 AI 投資循環仍具延續性。公司第二季營收與資料中心
業務均大幅優於預期，資料中心網路業務亦維持高速成長，顯示大型企業、雲端服務業者及 AI 模型
開發商對算力需求仍然強勁。同時，管理層對第三季營收及新一代 Vera Rubin 平台維持正向展望，
反映 AI 基礎建設建置並未出現明顯放緩跡象。不過，市場對 AI 產業的評價標準已逐漸由需求成長轉
向獲利品質與投資效益，未來資本支出、折舊攤提與現金流變化仍將是投資人關注重點。
從產業鏈角度來看，AI 需求正持續向外擴散。除了 GPU 之外，高速光通訊、網路交換器、高頻
寬記憶體(HBM)、CPU 及資料中心電力架構均成為下一階段重要受惠領域。另一方面，NVIDIA 近期
宣布結合金融機構與雲端服務資源打造大型 AI 基礎設施平台，顯示產業發展正由硬體銷售進一步延
伸至算力服務與平台營運模式。此舉有利強化 AI 產業生態系並提高資本取得效率，但也反映未來競
爭重點將由單純硬體性能逐步轉向平台整合能力、商業模式及資金運用效率。隨著更多企業投入 AI

建設，市場也開始關注高額資本支出是否能持續帶來相應回報，產業獲利表現仍可能出現明顯分化。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但市場關注焦點正由「建置多少算力」逐步
轉向「創造多少效益」。NVIDIA 新平台推廣進度、Vera Rubin 產品放量、高速光通訊滲透率提升
及 HBM 供給變化，將是觀察下一階段 AI 投資循環的重要指標。雖然大型雲端業者資本支出尚未出
現明顯下修，支撐整體供應鏈需求維持高檔，但隨著資料中心建置成本、能源支出與折舊費用持續增
加，企業獲利能力與現金流表現的重要性亦將提升。未來較具競爭優勢的企業，除了掌握核心技術外，
更需具備規模化商業模式及資本效率，才能在 AI 產業由高速成長邁向成熟發展的過程中持續創造價
值。

礦業類股：關稅預期有利銅價支撐，戰略庫存需求是潛在利多
8 月以來礦業類股走勢強勁，倫敦金屬交易所銅價也再創歷史新高，這波上漲的主要動能，仍是
來自於我們先前一再提到的美國潛在銅關稅，改變了全球銅庫存的流向。由於市場擔心美國未來可能
對精煉銅加徵關稅，交易商提前將銅運往美國，希望在關稅實施前完成進口，導致其他地區能夠取得
的現貨供應相應減少，在供給減少下持續為銅價帶來支撐。
截至 8 月底，美國現行關稅主要涵蓋銅棒、銅線、銅板及銅管等半成品與衍生產品，精煉銅尚未
正式納入課稅範圍，但市場普遍預估美國政府將有可能在 2027 年開始實施精煉銅關稅，帶動交易商

提前採取備貨行動。路透社報導指出，美國 2026 年上半年進口約 88.5 萬噸精煉銅，年增約 3%，紐
約商品交易所銅庫存也攀升至約 67.5 萬噸的歷史高點。若將時間拉長至一年觀察，全球三大交易所
合計增加約 49.58 萬噸庫存，其中約 81%集中於紐約商品交易所，顯示全球可見銅庫存雖然上升，
但新增庫存大多被鎖在美國，非美市場銅庫存相對處於較為緊張的狀態。
除了關稅帶動的庫存失衡外，銅的實際應用需求也具有基本面支撐。彭博行業研究預估，2026
年全球銅需求將成長 2.4%至 2.7%，增速雖較 2025 年放緩，但電動車、再生能源、資料中心及電網
升級仍將帶動用銅需求上升。其中雖然中國銅需求成長預估也將放緩，但是中國國家電網已規劃 2026
至 2030 年投資 4 兆元人民幣，較前五年增加 44%，以支應人工智慧用電需求。
另一方面，隨著區域銅庫存出現短缺及 AI 帶動用銅需求上升，若政府與企業在未來計畫提高安
全庫存，將可能成為市場尚未充分反映的中長期上漲基礎。花旗研究估算，目前全球精煉銅總庫存約

為 300 萬噸，僅相當於 1.3 個月的全球消費量。若各國政府及大型供應鏈基於國防、能源安全、人
工智慧基礎建設或地緣政治風險，將安全庫存提高至兩個月，市場約需要額外累積 170 萬噸銅庫存；
若提高至三個月，額外需求更將擴大至約 400 萬噸。由於新礦山從開發到投產需要多年，供給無法
在短期內迅速增加，市場只能透過銅價上漲，促使更多廢銅進入市場，同時迫使部分使用者節約用銅
或尋找替代材料。因此，建立庫存的規模愈大、完成時間愈短，銅價需要調整的漲幅也將愈顯著。
展望後市，儘管美國精煉銅關稅目前尚未正式實施，但市場預期帶來的提前備貨潮仍有利短線銅
價支撐，同時全球用銅需求仍維持成長，新礦山供給又難以快速實現，加上政府及企業可能逐步提高
安全庫存，使銅市場的中長期供需結構仍具有支撐，故對具銅價曝險的礦業類股中長期後市仍可正向
看待，然而仍需留意若未來銅關稅未如市場預期實施，當前囤積於美國市場的銅庫存將可能回歸到全
球市場當中，為銅價帶來短線波動。

### Page 9

身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
能源類股：荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，惟實際供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期
8 月以來，美伊衝突持續反覆升降溫，雙方時而釋出談判訊號，時而再度發生軍事衝突，短期內
仍未出現實質進展，荷莫茲海峽通行亦持續受限。然而，海峽受阻並不代表原有運量全數消失，部分
原油仍可經由海運與陸上替代管線出口，同時中國石油需求下降及各國政府釋放戰略庫存亦抵銷部分
中斷影響。
彭博行業研究指出，儘管當前荷莫茲海峽受到干擾，但實際並未呈現完全封鎖的狀態，目前仍有
零星油輪持續通行。彭博指出，由於部分油輪會關閉公開定位訊號，並在通過海峽後於阿曼外海進行
轉運，因此公開船舶資料可能會漏算部分運量。彭博綜合目前庫存變化與市場實際供需後，估計目前

每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油通過荷莫茲海峽，意味僅依據可觀察船舶數量推算中東石油供應中斷量，
可能高估了市場實際缺口。
除了每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油經過海峽運輸外，彭博估計目前另有每日約 500 萬桶石油改走陸
上管線；需求端則因中國每日減少約 400 萬桶進口而下降，其他地區減少用油與各國政府釋放戰略
庫存再合計填補每日約 200 萬桶石油缺口。經過上述調整後，原本每日約 2,000 萬桶經荷莫茲海峽
通行的運量中，實際供應缺口僅剩每日約 150 萬桶，這也解釋了當前油價漲幅為何未完全反映表面
的產量損失。
全球石油庫存變化的數據亦呼應了實際缺口低於市場普遍預期的論點。花旗報告指出，2026 年 2
月至 8 月全球實際觀察到的石油供需缺口平均約為每日 290 萬桶，明顯低於其原先預估的每日 700
萬至 800 萬桶。截至 2026 年 8 月，全球石油庫存仍可覆蓋 99 天需求，亦仍處於 2022 年至 2025
年的 95 至 101 天區間。花旗估計，若目前庫存下降速度延續，全球庫存要到 2029 年第一季才會降

至接近第二次石油危機緊張程度的 70 天水位，這顯示荷莫茲海峽若持續受阻，雖油價上漲壓力仍會
逐步累積，但實際庫存下降速度並未如市場普遍預期的嚴峻。
展望後市，除荷莫茲海峽中斷造成的供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期外，國際能源署也預估
2027 年全球石油需求將增加每日 240 萬桶，供應則可能年增每日 830 萬桶，意味若中東產量與海峽
通行逐步恢復，供應回升速度將明顯快於需求，並帶動庫存回補，不利油價上漲趨勢，然而考量短線
地緣風險仍有利能源類股形成支撐，在美伊達成長期停火協議前，應中性看待能源類股後市表現。

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：央行買盤支撐中長期價格，惟升息預期帶動短線波動升高
8 月金價走勢先揚後抑。月初公布的美國就業數據偏弱，加上中國及全球黃金 ETF 買盤回升，使
市場一度降低近期升息預期，金價因而突破 7 月的整理區間。到了 8 月中旬，美國財政部宣布擴大
長天期公債回購，帶動長端殖利率與美元回落，進一步放大黃金漲勢；不過，月底公布的通膨數據仍
顯示物價壓力具有黏性，聯準會主席華許在傑克森霍爾會議的態度也偏向鷹派，使市場重新提高升息
預期，金價隨即自高檔回落。然而即使月底出現修正，金價在 8 月仍累積明顯漲幅。
支撐金價中長期走勢的核心力量，仍然來自世界各國央行為分散外匯儲備，持續增持黃金。世界

黃金協會指出，2026 年第 2 季全球央行淨購金量為 289 噸，累計 2026 年上半年約 345 噸。雖然上
半年央行購金量受到土耳其、俄羅斯等國調節部位影響，降至 2022 年以來同期低點，但第 2 季購金
量已較第 1 季明顯回升，而且買盤仍分布於波蘭、中國、烏茲別克及哈薩克等多個市場，顯示各國央
行增持黃金的趨勢仍未出現轉變，且可觀察到各國央行對於逢低承接黃金的意願。
在央行需求之外，中國投資資金是本輪金價突破的重要邊際買盤。2026 年上半年中國黃金淨進
口量達 764 噸，較 2025 年同期增加 138%，其規模明顯高於中國人民銀行公布的增持量，顯示民間
與機構投資人也在提高黃金配置。當中國資金推動金價突破整理區間後，美國商品期貨交易委員會的
資料也顯示，管理基金黃金淨多單在 8 月持續增加，使原本集中於亞洲的漲勢進一步獲得美國期貨資
金支持。
然而，投資資金參與程度提高，也使金價對利率與美元變化更加敏感。美國財政部 8 月 19 日宣
布，將至少加倍 10 年至 30 年期公債的流動性支持回購規模，市場隨即下調長端殖利率，黃金也在

持有成本下降後快速上漲。不過，月底公布的美國 7 月通膨數據仍高，加上華許在傑克森霍爾會議維
持偏鷹立場，再次使市場重新認為聯準會仍可能透過升息抑制通膨，美元與殖利率回升後，部分短線
資金便出現獲利了結賣壓。
展望後市，各國央行增持黃金儲備仍支撐黃金的中長期多頭結構，而美國財政與債務可持續性疑
慮也將持續提高黃金的分散風險價值。不過，8 月底的回檔顯示，當金價漲勢由 ETF 及期貨資金放大
後，實質利率與美元只要轉為上升，短線市場部位便可能迅速調整，故在美國後續利率政策仍具有不
確定性的狀況下，對於黃金類股後市應中性看待。

固定收益：長端風險仍存，3~10 年期公債、投資級債更具表現機會
8 月以來，美國公債殖利率維持高檔震盪，若以 7 月底與 8/24 比較，各年期債券殖利率近乎平
行小幅下行。8 月初公布的 7 月非農就業意外減少 2.3 萬人，顯示勞動市場動能轉弱；7 月 CPI 年增
率由 3.5%降至 3.4%，核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.6%降至 2.5%，一度緩解市場對 Fed 緊縮疑慮。惟 7
月 PPI 與 PCE 數據表現，限制利率進一步下行空間。
8 月中旬之後，市場焦點由政策利率逐步轉向能源價格、財政供給與期限溢酬。美伊局勢反覆推

升油價，加上美國聯邦債務 8 月首度突破 40 兆美元，2 年與 30 年期利差由 7 月底 99 個基點擴大至
8/18 的 109 個基點，30 年期殖利率亦升至 5.28%。美國財政部 8/19 宣布，自 9/9 起將部分 10～
30 年期公債單次流動性回購上限由 20 億美元提高至至少 40 億美元，帶動 30 年期殖利率由 5.28%
快速回落至 5.19%。截至 8/24，2 年與 30 年利差亦回到 99 個基點，月中陡峭化大致回吐。此一走
勢顯示，回購措施雖有助改善長券流動性，但財政赤字、公債供給及期限溢酬等結構性壓力仍未消失。
全球主要成熟國家的長端債券亦面臨類似壓力。德國 30 年期公債殖利率近期升至約 3.79%，創
2011 年以來最高；日本 10 年期公債殖利率 8/18 盤中一度觸及 2.945%，亦為 1996 年 9 月以來高
位，30 年期亦一度升至 4.115%。成熟市場長債同步承壓，反映財政擴張、通膨風險與貨幣政策正常
化正共同推升長端期限溢酬。
不過，信用市場整體仍相對穩定，截至 8/24，聖路易聯準銀行的 ICE 美國銀行美國投資級公司

債 OAS 約 81 個基點、有效殖利率約 5.4%，但信用利差仍處歷史偏低區間，緩衝並不寬裕。AI 基礎
建設融資的供給壓力亦已開始反映在定價，Reuters 8/21 指出，2026 年至 8 月上旬主要 AI 雲端業
者發債規模已約 2,200 億美元，科技類投資級債利差升至 89 個基點，較整體 IG 市場高出約 9 個基
點；近期 Alphabet 新券亦需提供約 10～15 個基點讓利，顯示在企業基本面仍穩健之際，大量新券
供給已成為影響信用評價的重要因素。
展望 9 月，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔，若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利率
上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短存續期間亦有再投資風險；
10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。值得注意的是，截至 8/27，10 年與 30 年期殖利率
利差為 52 個基點，2 年至 30 年總利差 96 個基點，顯示 2 年至 30 年期殖利率曲線陡峭幅度中，逾
半來自 10~30 年區間，反映超長端相對 10 年期仍有明顯期限溢酬。相較之下，3～10 年期兼具較

高息收、適度存續期間與利率下行參與空間。整體而言，目前 3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風
險報酬條件相對均衡，料具表現機會。

Key Takeaways
We continue to see margin improvement, with October NPM at 23.4%, above
20.1% in 3Q26.
Margin improvement likely driven by combination of better pricing, improving
mix, and higher utilization for ABF and BT substrates.
Stay OW on both Unimicron and NYPCB – but on a 12-month view, we prefer
Unimicron (12.4x CY27e P/E) over NYPCB (13.0 CY27e P/E).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity

value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.3%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 17%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF and BT demand
n Faster-than-expected pickup in AI and 5G demand
n Stronger-than-expected ASP hike
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.

CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall, creating headwinds for ABF substrate demand and pricing
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 3

King Yuan Electronics Co (2449.TW)
25 November 2026 Citi Research
3
King Yuan Electronics Co
Valuation
Our target price for KYEC of NT$286 is derived using a DCF methodology. Using Bloomberg data, we apply a risk-free rate of
4%, a risk premium of 5%, and a beta of 1.4x for a 10.8% cost of equity. With 20% corporate tax and a 3.0% interest rate on its
debt, our WACC for KYEC is 9.7%. We assume terminal sales growth of 5%. We model its adjusted EBIT margin to gradually

improve from 23% in 2024 to c30% in 2035E. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027/28E P/B of 4x/4x and
2027/28E P/E of 25x/17x, in line with its global peers.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent KYEC shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected end-
demand for global smartphone/consumer electronic products; 2) slower ramp for AI-related products; 3) slower-than-
expected demand recovery in the automotive and base station sector; and 4) pricing pressure on increasing competition.

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 3

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Victory Giant is a leading AI PCB supplier, with continued investment in R&D and 
capacity expansion to support long-term growth. We are positive on the company’s 
growth prospects, driven by rising AI infrastructure demand, PCB specification upgrades 
toward higher layer counts and higher-end materials, increasing PCB usage in AI servers 
to replace copper cables, and customer expansion into ASIC servers. We expect 
migration to next-generation AI platforms to support higher dollar content, while 
continued capacity expansion should enable the company to capture growing demand. 
We are Buy-rated on Victory Giant. 
We are Buy-rated on Shennan. The company is a key PCB supplier in China that focuses 
on high-end markets, including, telecom, datacom, automotive, industrials, medicals, 
etc. We see the company as a key beneficiary of China’s domestic AI infrastructure 
investments, and therefore expect a robust revenue and net profit growth trend for 
Shennan as local equipment vendors ramp up their server/networking shipment. Current 
valuation is attractive as it trades at a discount to historical average. 
WUS is a leading global PCB supplier, expanding from telecom equipment, networking 
devices, to high-speed networking in AI data center: 800G / 1.6T switch PCB, switch 
board in rack-level AI servers, midplane / backplane in rack-level AI servers to replace 
copper cables, etc. We are positive on WUS growth ahead, driven by (1) AI infrastructure 
ramp up, (2) PCB specification upgrade along with rising computing density per rack and 
high-speed networking, (3) PCB usage increase per AI servers rack, replacing copper 
cables, easier for assembly, and (4) strong commitment in both R&D and capacity 
expansion, supporting the company’s ability to capture the rising PCB trend. We are 
Buy-rated on WUS. WUS currently trades at 15.8x 2027E P/E and we believe the 
valuation is attractive considering +48% NI YoY on avg. in 2027-28E and OPM up to 26% 
by 2028E from 24% in 9M25.  
Shengyi Tech is a leading copper-clad laminate (CCL) supplier and a key beneficiary of 
growing AI infrastructure demand. We are positive on the company’s long-term growth, 
driven by rising AI server shipments, CCL specification upgrades and continued capacity 
expansion. We expect higher transmission speeds in rack-level AI servers to support 
increasing adoption of M9 and above products, driving product mix improvement, while

strong demand should support AI CCL pricing. We believe AI CCL demand growth can
absorb planned industry capacity additions, supporting continued shipment growth and

Valuation: We derive our 12M TP of Rmb540 on a target P/E multiple of 26.3x applied
to our 2027E EPS. Our target P/E of 26.3x is derived from the correlation between P/E
and EPS growth of Victory Giant’s peers, based on the company’s 2028E EPS YoY

growth.
Key risks: Slower-than-expected AI server shipment ramp up pace;
Slower-than-expected AI server PCB specification upgrade; Fiercer-than-expected
market competition.
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Shennan Circuits
We are Buy rated on Shennan Circuits with a 12-month target price of Rmb511, which is
based on a 39.7x target P/E multiple on our 2027E EPS.
Downside risks: 1) slower-than-expected expansion to AI PCB; 2) fiercer-than-expected

competition, which could lead to ASP erosion and margin pressure; 3) customer
concentration risk; and 4) slower-than-expected server/automotive PCB and IC
substrate growth.
Valuation: We derive our 12M TP of Rmb148 on a target P/E multiple of 23x 2027E EPS.
Our target P/E of 23x is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of
WUS’s peers, based on the company’s 2027E-28E EPS YoY growth.
Key risks: (1) Slower-than-expected high-end AI server and high-speed switch

migration; (2) Fiercer-than-expected competition in AI PCB market; and (3)
Slower-than-expected new capacity expansion.
Valuation: We derive our 12M TP of Rmb255 on a target P/E multiple of 50.4x 2027E
EPS. Our target P/E of 50.4x is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth
of Shengyi Tech’s peers, based on the company’s 2027E-28E EPS YoY growth.
Key risks: Lower-than-expected AI infrastructure investment, Lower-than-expected
allocation, Change in the direction of technology.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

3324.TT 雙鴻 
 4Q26 新品進入量產

[法說會更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、伺服器等領域，伺服器主要終端客戶為美系 Tier 1 
CSP，消費性電子客戶包含 Apple (AAPL US)、Dell (DELL US)、HP (HPQ US)、宏碁
(2353)、華碩(2357)等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,260 
【前次投資建議】買進，1,775 
【大盤指數/股價】40195 / 979

➢ 3Q26AI 新舊品交替、消費性產品疲弱，毛利率低於預期  
雙鴻 3Q26 營收 QoQ+12.5%，YoY+64.3%，毛利率 25.7%，營益率
13.9%，EPS 為 11.59 元，毛利率、獲利低於投研部預期。公司 3Q26 營
收季增、年增，是受惠於 GB300 的加單效應，3Q26 伺服器營收占比已
經達總營收的 81%，消費性產品則是市況疲弱；因消費性產品之產品組
合不佳，且 AI 伺服器處於新舊品轉換期，伺服器有多項新設計進行試
產，導致毛利率 QoQ-2.8ppts，YoY-4.1ppts，連帶使營益率下滑。 
➢ 2027 年伺服器營收目標占比 90% 
雙鴻 10 月營收 33.7 億元 MoM+16.9%，YoY+44.7%，4Q26 有 Vera 
Rubin 與 ASIC 散熱新產品進入量產階段，若放量進度順利，受惠於營
運規模擴大，OPEX%將是下降趨勢，毛利率亦有望回升，營運動能可優
於 3Q26，投研部預估雙鴻 4Q26 營收 114.7 億元 QoQ+17.2%，
YoY+50.95%，毛利率 27.6%，營益率 16.5%，EPS 為 15.36 元。 
目前 AI 伺服器晶片所需的解熱效能約 3000W，根據半導體業者的晶片
設計規劃，2027~2028 年會往 6000W 發展，帶動散熱產品規格升級。
雙面水冷板(Double-Sided Cold Plate) ASP 約為單面產品的 1.5 倍。此
外，下一世代 AI 伺服器會使用 MCL (Micro-channel Lid)，雙鴻已配合
客戶開發中。雙鴻表示，2027 年 ASIC 伺服器成長性很大，公司的伺服
器營收占比可望進一步拉升至 90%，因產品複雜度升級，毛利率也能優
於 2026 年。  
➢ 建議逢低買進，目標價 1,260 元  
雙鴻 3Q26 毛利率低於預期，若 4Q26 Vera Rubin、ASIC 新品放量順
利，投研部認為 4Q26~1H27 公司的營運動能將逐漸轉強。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X，平均 PE 多位於 15X~20X，近期 AI 伺服器產業利空
頻傳，股價動能集體受到壓抑，考量雙鴻 PE 已接近下緣，美方加嚴 AI
審查也不等於全面停止 AI DC 建置，AI 仍是具有成長性的產業，建議逢
低買進，目標價 1,260 元(2027F PE 15X)。

個股報告

3Q26AI 新舊品交替、消費性產品疲弱，毛利率低於預期 
雙鴻 3Q26 營收 QoQ+12.5%，YoY+64.3%，毛利率 25.7%，營益率 13.9%，EPS 為
11.59 元，毛利率、獲利低於投研部預期。公司 3Q26 營收季增、年增，是受惠於 GB300
的加單效應，3Q26 伺服器營收占比已經達總營收的 81%，消費性產品則是市況疲弱；
因消費性產品之產品組合不佳，且 AI 伺服器處於新舊品轉換期，伺服器有多項新設計
進行試產，導致毛利率 QoQ-2.8ppts，YoY-4.1ppts，連帶使營益率下滑。 
圖 1：雙鴻產品組合 2021~2026F

資料來源：國票投顧 
2027 年伺服器營收目標占比 90% 
雙鴻 10 月營收 33.7 億元 MoM+16.9%，YoY+44.7%，4Q26 有 Vera Rubin 與 ASIC
散熱新產品進入量產階段，若放量進度順利，受惠於營運規模擴大，OPEX%將是下降
趨勢，毛利率亦有望回升，營運動能可優於 3Q26，投研部預估雙鴻 4Q26 營收 114.7
億元 QoQ+17.2%，YoY+50.95%，毛利率 27.6%，營益率 16.5%，EPS 為 15.36 元。 
目前 AI 伺服器晶片所需的解熱效能約 3000W，根據半導體業者的晶片設計規劃，
2027~2028 年會往 6000W 發展，帶動散熱產品規格升級。雙面水冷板(Double-Sided 
Cold Plate)ASP 約為單面產品的 1.5 倍。此外，下一世代 AI 伺服器會使用 MCL (Micro-
channel Lid)，雙鴻已配合客戶開發中。雙鴻表示，2027 年 ASIC 伺服器成長性很大，
公司的伺服器營收占比可望進一步拉升至 90%，因產品複雜度升級，毛利率也能優於
2026 年。 
2027 年泰國擴產進度不變 
近期 AI 伺服器產業充斥利空消息：Tier 1 CSP 自由現金流轉負(投資人質疑 AI 投資的
變現能力)、NVIDIA 執行長黃仁勳下台恐影響未來 GPU 開發進度、美國民主黨可能
加嚴對 AI DC 建置/AI 晶片進出口之審查等，市場憂心 AI 發展將放緩。雙鴻認為，各
家 CSP 會持續建置算力，如算力不足，將輸掉 AI 競賽，就目前訂單來看，2027 年 AI
伺服器水冷散熱市場樂觀，公司 2026、2027 年 CAPEX 都預計達 40~50 億元，對泰
國的工廠建設維持原訂計畫。

個股報告 
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠布局以 AI 伺服器
水冷散熱為重點：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold、
Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/月、manifold 3k/月；2)泰
國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產 cold plate、manifold，並預留電源水冷
產能，若各產品線產能順利拉升，未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍；3)泰國三期規劃在
4Q26 動工、2H27 投產，負責生產 cold plate、CDU、Rack；4)泰國四期預計 2028
年動工；5)中國廠區負責生產筆電、高階顯示卡、伺服器散熱相關產品，整體上，以
氣冷模組較多，也有少量的 cold plate、manifold；6)台灣主力生產 CDU (Cooling 
Distribution Unit)，並負責高階與高精度液冷產品的研發和樣品試產。 
建議逢低買進，目標價 1,260 元 
雙鴻 3Q26 毛利率低於預期，若 4Q26 Vera Rubin、ASIC 新品放量順利，投研部認
為 4Q26~1H27 公司的營運動能將逐漸轉強。雙鴻歷史 PE 區間為 8X~36X，平均 PE
多位於 15X~20X，近期 AI 伺服器產業利空頻傳，股價動能集體受到壓抑，考量雙鴻
PE 已接近下緣，美方加嚴 AI 審查也不等於全面停止 AI DC 建置，AI 仍是具有成長性
的產業，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,260 元(2027F PE 15X)。 
資料來源：CMoney、國票投顧

Document 260: AI发现物理学“大一统理论”？斯坦福学者联手GPT-6 Astra，提出量子引力新框架

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI发现物理学“大一统理论”？斯坦福学者联手GPT-6 Astra，提出量子引力新框架.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

斯坦福⼤学物理学家与OpenAI GPT-6 Astra合作数⽉，提出⼀套四维量⼦引⼒数学框架，
累计消耗约4亿个token。论⽂称，该框架解决了传统计算中的发散问题，复现⿊洞熵公
式，并在低能极限下还原爱因斯坦场⽅程。不过，论⽂尚未经过同⾏评审，理论可靠性及
AI的实际贡献仍待验证。
摘要：
AI正在从科研助⼿⾛向基础科学研究的深⽔区。⼀项由斯坦福⼤学物理学家与OpenAI GPT-6
Astra合作开展的量⼦引⼒研究，引发了科技与物理学界关注。不少⽹友将其称为“AI发现⼤⼀统
理论”，认为这可能为统⼀⼴义相对论与量⼦⼒学带来新的突破。
11⽉25⽇，斯坦福⼤学莱因⻙伯理论物理研究所物理学家Venkatesa Chandrasekaran在arXiv发
布⼀篇83⻚的预印本论⽂，提出了⼀套在四维时空中实现引⼒背景独⽴、⾮微扰量⼦化的数学
框架。
论⽂致谢部分披露，研究中的若⼲计算与证明来⾃与OpenAI GPT-6 Astra⻓达数⽉的深度协
作，累计消耗约4亿个token。
论⽂声称，这套新框架不仅解决了传统量⼦引⼒计算中的发散问题，还证明有限区域的量⼦态

数量有限，复现了⿊洞熵公式，并在低能极限下还原经典爱因斯坦场⽅程。
如果这些结果能够经受独⽴验证，将为量⼦引⼒研究提供⼀条新的数学路径，也展现出AI参与
前沿基础科学研究的潜⼒。不过，该论⽂⽬前尚未经过同⾏评审，相关结论仍有待物理学界检
验。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

AI深度参与：数⽉协作，消耗4亿Token
相⽐理论本⾝，AI在这项研究中扮演的⻆⾊同样引⼈关注。
论⽂致谢部分明确提及，GPT-6 Astra参与了研究中的若⼲计算与证明⼯作。作者与模型进⾏了
⻓达数⽉的深度协作，累计消耗约4亿个token。此外，论⽂还感谢OpenAI CEO Sam Altman及
Sébastien Bubeck等⼈的⽀持。
这⼀合作模式与过去AI辅助科研的⽅式有所不同。此前，AI更多承担数学推导、代码编写和特定
问题求解等任务，⽽此次合作围绕⼀套完整的量⼦引⼒研究框架展开，涉及持续数⽉的计算、
理论推导与证明⼯作。
今年10⽉，⼀项由约30位物理学家参与的基准测试显⽰，GPT-6 Astra在50道未发表的研究级
理论物理题⽬中答对49道，题⽬涵盖⿊洞物理与全息理论等领域。这些⽅向与此次论⽂⾼度相
关，也为理解AI参与前沿理论物理研究的能⼒提供了参照。
不过，论⽂并未详细披露哪些核⼼计算和证明由GPT-6 Astra完成，因此⽬前还⽆法准确评估模
型对最终理论成果的实际贡献。

### Page 3

百年难题：为什么引⼒⽆法被顺利量⼦化？
⼴义相对论和量⼦⼒学是现代物理学的两⼤⽀柱，但两套理论描述世界的⽅式存在根本差异。
量⼦⼒学描述微观世界的量⼦规律，⼴义相对论则认为，引⼒本质上是时空⼏何的弯曲。当物
理学家试图将引⼒纳⼊量⼦理论时，⾸先遇到的就是数学发散问题。
在传统微扰量⼦引⼒框架下，研究⼈员将引⼒⼦视为在平直时空中传播的粒⼦，通过逐阶计算
量⼦修正来描述引⼒相互作⽤。然⽽，⾼能循环计算会产⽣⽆法通过有限数量参数消除的紫外
⽆穷⼤发散，即所谓的“不可重整化”问题，导致理论难以建⽴稳定的预测体系。
另⼀个难点是背景独⽴性。⼴义相对论中的时空并⾮固定不变的舞台，⽽是能够受到物质和能
量影响的动态结构。因此，真正的量⼦引⼒理论不能简单地预设固定时空背景。
过去⼏⼗年，弦理论、圈量⼦引⼒和AdS/CFT对应关系等⽅案都试图解决这⼀问题，但各⾃⾯
临不同的理论局限，量⼦引⼒⾄今仍没有形成获得⼴泛认可的完整答案。
核⼼⽅案：从光锥边界重建量⼦引⼒
与传统⽅法不同，Chandrasekaran选择从光锥结构⼊⼿。

光锥描述了时空中某⼀事件能够影响其他事件的因果范围。两个事件的前向与后向光锥相交，
可以围出⼀个有限区域，称为「因果钻⽯」。
论⽂的核⼼设想是：因果钻⽯内部的引⼒信息，可以通过其光锥边界上的信息完整描述。
作者利⽤引⼒场⽅程在光锥边界上的特殊性质，提取出不依赖固定时空背景的约化相空间，再
将经典泊松括号转化为量⼦算符的对易关系，利⽤⾼斯波函数构建⾮微扰的希尔伯特空间表
⽰。
与传统⽅法需要逐阶展开引⼒相互作⽤不同，这⼀框架试图直接在有限⽜顿引⼒常数下处理量
⼦引⼒，从⽽绕开传统计算中的发散问题。
此外，作者将参考系锚定在光锥交汇处的⼏何结构上，并利⽤光沿光锥传播的距离作为内禀时
钟。多个因果钻⽯还可以通过共享边界拼接，构建整个理论的路径积分。
四项关键成果：从消除发散到复现⿊洞熵
论⽂声称，这套新框架在四个⽅⾯取得了重要进展。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
第⼀，传统计算中的⽆穷⼤发散得到解决。作者计算了光锥上引⼒⼦对的产⽣与湮灭振幅。传
统微扰论会得到对数发散，⽽新框架通过⾮微扰求和，得到了有限的闭合解析表达式，并声称
所有中间态的物理求和均绝对收敛。
第⼆，有限时空区域的信息容量得到约束。论⽂证明，有界因果钻⽯对应的希尔伯特空间维度
有限，⾯积算符具有离散谱。这与全息原理相呼应，即⼀个空间区域所能容纳的信息量受到边
界⾯积⽽⾮体积的限制。
第三，复现⻉肯斯坦-霍⾦⿊洞熵公式。论⽂通过对⿊洞边际俘获⾯上的量⼦态进⾏求和，得到
了⻉肯斯坦-霍⾦⾯积熵公式。作者还计算了⼤型史⽡西⿊洞的⼀圈量⼦修正，复现了印度物理
学家Ashoke Sen此前利⽤欧⼏⾥得路径积分⽅法得到的对数修正系数。两种不同数学⽅法得出
相同结果，为新框架提供了⼀项⼀致性检验。

第四，经典爱因斯坦场⽅程在低能极限下重新涌现。论⽂声称，在宏观低能极限下，该框架能
够在相⼲初态的⼀圈近似⽔平上还原经典半经典爱因斯坦场⽅程。这意味着，新理论试图解释
量⼦引⼒如何过渡到宏观世界熟悉的经典时空。
距离真正突破仍有多远？
尽管论⽂提出了颇具雄⼼的理论框架，但距离获得物理学界认可，仍有不少障碍。
⾸先，论⽂⽬前只是⼀篇未经同⾏评审的预印本。量⼦引⼒领域并不缺少数学上看似⾃洽、但
最终难以通过严格检验的理论⽅案。据报道，Chandrasekaran在伯克利进⾏线下学术报告时，
已经遭遇学者密集的质疑与反驳。
其次，这套框架⽬前仅覆盖纯真空引⼒，尚未纳⼊电⼦、夸克等物质场。如何将标准模型中的
基本粒⼦及其相互作⽤整合进来，仍是⼀项重⼤挑战。
第三，实验验证依然极其困难。量⼦引⼒效应通常只有在普朗克能标附近才会变得显著，远超
现有粒⼦对撞机的实验能⼒。未来研究⼈员可能需要从极早期宇宙、宇宙微波背景辐射等观测
数据中寻找间接证据。
此外，论⽂没有明确划分⼈类研究者与GPT-6 Astra各⾃的贡献。AI能够参与计算和证明，并不

意味着其已经独⽴提出或验证了整套理论。理论框架的物理意义、数学严谨性以及能否与现实
世界相符，仍需要⼈类科学家进⾏独⽴审查。
从统⼀⼴义相对论与量⼦⼒学，到解释⿊洞熵和时空的信息容量，量⼦引⼒研究始终是基础物
理学最艰难的前沿之⼀。这⼀次，AI深度参与了⼀项持续数⽉的理论研究，但这套框架究竟能否
成为量⼦引⼒研究的新⽅向，仍有待同⾏评审和后续研究给出答案。
限免

Document 313: Data centres power a broader non-mining capex surge (2026-11-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Data centres power a broader non-mining capex surge (2026-11-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-28
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #construction
Data centres power a broader non-mining capex surge
By Pat Bustamante & Luka Belobrajdic
15:43 November 26 2026 Share
Private capex surged in the September quarter, rising 12.2%qtr – the strongest read since
the height of the mining boom in the September quarter of 2011. Machinery and equipment
rocketed to its strongest quarterly result on record, led by a return to form in information,
media and telecommunications, while buildings and structures too provided support. Capex

expectations point to further growth ahead.

### Page 2

Private capex surprised to the upside, rising 12.2%qtr in the September quarter of 2026. Following a
sizeable 1.6ppt upward revision to the June quarter result, annual growth accelerated to 18.6%yr.
Today’s outcome exceeded even our top-of-market expectation for a 10.0%qtr rise and was well ahead
of market consensus of 4.3%qtr.
Today’s rise was primarily driven by strength across machinery and equipment (M&E), which posted its
strongest quarterly increase on record, up 23.1%qtr and 34.3%yr. Buildings and structures (B&S)

increased 1.7%qtr and 4.5%yr, providing a solid base of support to the headline. Looking across
sectors, the non-mining economy is leading the charge, with capex surging and mining investment also
providing some support.
Data centres power the non-mining investment surge
As we have previously highlighted, there is an investment surge underway in the non-mining economy.
The epicentre is the data-centre build-out, but the surge extends well beyond data centres themselves

Investment is rising in energy and renewables needed to power new data centres, while services
industries (including finance and other business services) are investing heavily in equipment to take
advantage of the AI revolution. Construction and rental services investment is also rising strongly,
supporting the broader build-out. Indeed, non-mining capex surged 16%qtr and 24%yr, the strongest
year-ended growth ever, outside the rebound from the early-1990s recession.

And there is more to come. Expectations data suggest non-mining capex could rise by around 20% in
year-average terms in 2026–27, which would be the strongest nominal growth on record, even stronger
than the 15% recorded in 2025-26. Importantly, capex estimate 4 already incorporates one quarter of
actual spending, making it a better guide to where investment is ultimately likely to land.
Some of this strength reflects higher prices for electronic equipment. But even after adjusting for

inflation, non-mining capex is expected to grow by around 17% in year-average terms in 2026–27, the
second strongest rate on record, and that comes on top of an already strong 2025–26 (13.6% in year
average terms).
And while non-mining investment is leading the charge, mining investment is also showing signs of life,
rising 1.4%qtr and 3.8%yr. Expectations suggest the sector will continue investing at around this pace
over the 2026-27 financial year.

### Page 3

What does this mean for the economy?
Despite the high import content of data-centre investment, we estimate that around half of the spending
will ultimately flow through to domestic activity, somewhat higher than the RBA’s estimate of around
one-third. Imported equipment still needs to be transported and installed, while the data centres
themselves require structures to house the equipment and supporting infrastructure. Our estimate is
based on input-output analysis that captures these domestic supply-chain linkages. The investment

surge is therefore providing a meaningful boost to activity and incomes, partly offsetting weakness
elsewhere in the economy.
Over the longer term, a larger and more modern capital stock should lift productive capacity and
productivity. But in the near term, the investment surge is primarily a boost to demand, partly offsetting
weakness elsewhere in the economy.
What does this mean for next week’s National Accounts?
Combined with yesterday’s construction data, today’s numbers point to stronger growth in business

investment and construction activity over the quarter, suggesting some upside to our Q3 estimate
(currently sitting at 0.5%qtr). However, with several important partial indicators still to come, we will
provide our full assessment in tomorrow’s GDP preview.
What about inflationary pressures?

### Page 4

Capex price pressures accelerated further, with the headline deflator rising 1.7%qtr, the strongest result
since June 2023 and up from 0.7%qtr in the June quarter. The M&E deflator rose a sharp 3.1%qtr,
compared with an average decline of 0.1%qtr over the previous three years, lifting six-month
annualised growth to 3.3%. The turnaround likely reflects broader inflationary pressures and a softer
Australian dollar, particularly given the import-intensive nature of the renewed surge in data-centre

equipment investment.
Industry detail
Mining capex continues to support the headline, rising 1.4%qtr, and moving 3.8% higher in year-ended
terms. Machinery and equipment led the increase, up 3.2%qtr, while buildings and structures edged
0.5%qtr higher. Non-mining capex rose 16.0%qtr and remains 24.0% higher over the year. The rise
was driven by machinery and equipment, up 27.1%qtr, while buildings and structures rose 2.4%qtr.
As anticipated, information, media and telecommunications led the capex surge, rising 86.7%qtr.

Leading indicators had pointed to renewed strength, with ‘other commercial’ building approvals
remaining highly elevated through H1 2026 and ADP equipment imports reaching another record high
in the September quarter, rising 83.9%qtr. B&S rose 8.9%qtr, while M&E surged 183.5%qtr, rebounding
sharply from last quarter’s 51.1% decline and highlighting the inherent lumpiness of data-centre
equipment investment.

### Page 5

Looking through these quarterly swings, capex in the industry remains 142.7% higher over the year
and is the second-largest source of investment behind mining. Its growing contribution to aggregate
investment is increasingly reminiscent of the early stages of the mining investment boom, with
movements in the sector continuing to dominate headline capex outcomes.
Importantly, the non-mining investment surge extends beyond data centres. Financial and insurance

capex rose 16.7%qtr, with gains across B&S (+32.6%qtr) and M&E (+14.0%qtr). Electricity, gas, water
and waste services rose 7.0%qtr and 20.8%yr to a new quarterly high, led by B&S (+7.7%qtr), while
M&E was flat. The industry should remain supported by the energy transition and infrastructure
requirements associated with data-centre expansion. Retail trade also reached a new quarterly high
following a 6.1%qtr rise, further underscoring the breadth of strength across non-mining investment.

Capex spending plans
Alongside the capex data, the ABS also publishes businesses spending plans for this financial year,
which allows us to assess how the capex spending is likely to evolve over the coming quarters. Usually,
those plans tend to have a bias, with companies first estimates usually erring on the side of caution.
Over time, business tend to revise their plans higher. We apply a methodology, that takes historical
ratios of plans to actuals alongside business conditions which allows us to derive a more realistic

capex estimate in the year ahead.
Estimate 4 for FY2027 came in at $223.7bn, slightly above our expectation of $221bn. Spending

### Page 6

intentions were 17.1% above the equivalent estimate a year ago and were revised 11.8% higher from
Estimate 3 just three months earlier, underscoring the strength of the investment pipeline. Based on the
historical profile of estimates, this implies nominal capex of around $253bn in FY2027, representing
growth of 20.5%yr.
After a year of relatively subdued outcomes, price pressures are beginning to re-emerge, with the
capex deflator rising 1.7%yr. We expect some further acceleration through the remainder of 2026, with

the recent depreciation of the Australian dollar placing upward pressure on the price of imported capital
goods, particularly given the import-intensive nature of data-centre investment. Combined with nominal
growth of 20.5%yr, this implies real capex growth of around 15.8%yr, which would be the strongest
outcome since the mining investment boom in FY2012.

### Page 1

economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
IN BRIEF
A week richer in geopolitical and market developments than macroe-
conomic ones:
19 November
While President Trump held warm meetings with Presidents Xi and
Putin in China (bilaterally and trilaterally, with no deliverables an-
nounced), 30+ country leaders gathered in Berlin at the Coalition of the

Willing Summit, hosted by German Chancellor Merz and co-chaired
with French President Macron and UK Prime Minister Burnham — to
pledge continued support to Ukraine, financial and otherwise.
20 November
The EU and UK had a successful Brexit reset summit yielding concrete
steps toward a closer economic relationship. Tech stocks led global
markets into a tailspin. The S&P 500 is down more than 10% from its
peak, and the Nasdaq more than 20%, implying negative returns year

to date. It lost $4tn of market capitalisation over the week, of which
half on Friday alone.
These moves, should they be sustained, let alone worsen, could call
into question the picture of economic strength and hawkish central
banks that has prevailed until now (including in the write-up below).
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
EUROZONE
Tame wage growth confirms rate outlook; new ECB leadership pac-
kage set. Negotiated wages rose 2.6% y/y in Q3. The labour cost index

grew faster (3.4%), but this partly reflects higher employment during
the period, which is a positive development. That said, ECB officials’
statements increasingly converged on the need for further tightening
as October inflation was confirmed at 4% y/y, and core at 2.6%. Ger-
many nominated Princeton academic Markus Brunnermeier to succeed
Philip Lane as ECB chief economist, while France nominated former Eu-
rope Minister Laurence Boone to succeed Isabel Schnabel. EU leaders

should formally endorse them, along with Pablo Hernández de Cos as
President, at their Summit next week. For a commentary on what this
means and the priorities that await them, see Isabelle Mateos y Lago’s
Substack and Editorial).
France
Mixed economic data, a surprise budget golden rule proposal, and
2027 electoral map starting to take shape. The INSEE business climate
index fell from 97 in October to 95 in November, with accommodation,
catering and retail particularly affected following the October riots.

Manufacturing proved more resilient, with its index at 104, above both
its 2026 and long-term average. Business creations were up 14% y/y
in October and 16.5% over the latest three months. Growth in bank
and market financing to businesses slowed respectively to 4.4% and
3.6% y/y in September (with the average cost of market financing ex-
ceeding that of bank financing by 60 bps, the most since December
2022).
On Sunday 22 November, President Macron surprised by announcing a

plan to convene the National Assembly and Senate in a special session
in early January, to vote on a constitutional reform initiated in 2011
that would require to balance the budget over time. This would require
a special majority of 3/5ths of the Congress, which arithmetically exists
based on parties that have recently proposed a fiscal rule. However,
success is far from assured given political dynamics. The passage of
this reform would go a long way to appease market fears over the

direction of French fiscal policy beyond the 2027 elections. Meanwhile,
Budget Minister Amiel said on Nov. 20 that France must find at least
€6 billion of additional savings in the 2027 budget after growth slum-
ped and the government made additional spending pledges. This is
consistent with the latest European Commission forecast that France's
budget deficit will reach 5.5% of GDP in 2026, exceeding the revised
5.4% target, and will barely narrow in 2027.

On the election front, with five months to go before the first round,
3 of the 4 leading candidates of the right and centre (David Lisnard,
Bruno Retailleau and Gabriel Attal) have opened discussions on a pos-
sible right-and-centre primary election at the end of January. Édouard
Philippe has ruled out taking part in it. Meanwhile, the National Rally
(Rassemblement national) has presented its long-awaited proposals
for pension reform. They include Marine Le Pen’s long-made promise

to lower the legal retirement age to 62, but with several measures
aimed at containing the costs of the reform. Also included is the crea-
tion of a mandatory capitalisation pillar. Despite these mitigating steps,
most analysts (ourselves included) expect the measures to have a net
negative impact on fiscal sustainability. For a synthetic primer on the
candidates’ fiscal and pension proposals, see our Focus.
Upcoming: INSEE household confidence (Wednesday); November HICP

inflation flash estimate, Q3 employment and final Q3 GDP (Friday).
Germany
Sentiment rebounds, but producer-price pressures accelerate shar-
ply. The ZEW economic confidence index jumped 7.5 points to 35.6 in
November, its highest since February 2026, while the assessment of
current conditions also improved, albeit remaining deeply negative.
Producer prices, meanwhile, surged 8.1% y/y and 1.4% m/m in October,
their fastest annual increase since February 2023, driven primarily by

energy and energy-intensive sectors.
Upcoming: November Ifo Business Survey (Tuesday); Q3 National Ac-
counts (Wednesday); November GfK Consumer Sentiment (Thursday).
Italy
Energy pushes inflation to a three-year high. Harmonised inflation
reached 5.6% y/y in October, the highest since September 2023, al-
though core inflation was considerably more contained, at 2.3%. Energy
inflation reached 31.8%, while goods prices rose 7.6% against 3.0% for
services.

UNITED KINGDOM
Consumers perk up even as inflation and labour-market strains
persist. Payroll employment fell by 34,000 in October, extending the
12-month decline to 95,000, and vacancies dropped to 690,000, their
lowest since 2014. The LFS measure was more encouraging, showing
employment up 156,000 in the three months to September and unem-
ployment edging down to 4.6%. Inflation accelerated slightly to 3.6%
y/y, while GfK consumer confidence recovered two points to -22 and

October retail-sales volumes rebounded 0.7% m/m after falling 0.6%
in September.
The Brexit reset inches forward. At a Summit on Friday hailed as a
reset of the UK-EU relationship, the two sides agreed on a series of
measures aimed at easing food trade, linking carbon markets and faci-
litating youth mobility. The implementation plan will also include coo-
peration on the EU’s “Made in Europe” initiative, potentially improving
UK firms’ access to EU procurement opportunities. UK Prime Minis-

ter Burnham is explicitly casting this agreement as the first step of a

### Page 2

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
journey to reconsider Brexit. He intends to set out options that could
include rejoining the customs union or single market or possibly the
EU itself, although this is controversial even within his own party and
would in any case only happen in the next Parliament.
UNITED STATES
Resilient activity, struggling housing market and more Fed hikes

ahead. October headline activity data were soft, but the details held
up better. Industrial production fell 0.4% m/m, largely because of a
4.3% drop in utilities, while manufacturing output rose 0.3% m/m and
its annual growth reached 2.1%, the fastest since 2022. Retail sales
slowed to 0.2% m/m, but the GDP-relevant control group accelerated
to 0.6%. Housing remains the weak spot: starts rebounded 9.9% after
three months of sharp declines, but permits fell 7.5% and the NAHB

homebuilder index dropped to its lowest since 2012. Minutes from the
27-28 October FOMC meeting showed that “almost all participants”
expected further tightening under their baseline outlook and FOMC
members comments this week confirmed these views, albeit with disa-
greement over the pace and scale of extra tightening needed. We have
added one rate hike in 2027 to our earlier forecast, to take the Fed Fund
rate to 4.5-4.75% by mid-year.
Upcoming: Q3 GDP second estimate, Fed Beige Book, October PCE and

core PCE inflation, October durable-goods orders and new-home sales
(Wednesday).
JAPAN
Inflation moves further above target. Headline CPI rose 0.8% m/m and
2.3% y/y in October as energy prices increased for the first time since
July. Underlying pressures strengthened as the new core measure ex-
cluding fresh food and energy accelerated to 0.5% m/m. BoJ Governor
Kazuo Ueda expressed confidence that consumption would rebound
after disappointing Q3 GDP and reiterated that raising the policy rate

remained warranted.
TO WATCH NEXT WEEK
Major economies: Flash PMIs for November (Monday).
EMERGING ECONOMIES
ASIA
The AI-led equity market sell-off is spreading.
- Hong Kong Hang Seng Index: -5.2% between 13 November and 20 No-
vember;
- China CSI 300 Index: -1.5% between 13 November and 20 November
(of which IT subindex: -5.5%);
- South Korea Kospi Index: -8% between 13 November and 20 No-
vember and -30.1% since 1 October;
- Taiwan TWSE: -17.5% between 13 November and 20 November and

-25.5% since 1 October. The KRW and TWD have held up.
Southeast Asia is still one of the world’s strongest growth spots. Over
the first nine months of 2026, Vietnam led the region with 9% growth,
followed by Singapore at 6% and Malaysia at 5.6%, while Thailand and
the Philippines lagged with GDP growing at 2.8% and 2.5%. The diver-
gence largely reflects the stronger boost from AI-related investment
and semiconductor and electronics exports in the more technolo-

gy-oriented economies.
LATIN AMERICA
Brazil
Slower activity and fiscal doubts put the real under renewed pressure.
The central bank’s activity index contracted 0.9% q/q in Q3 amid tight
monetary conditions. The Senate approved a fiscal package expected to
improve the primary balance by 0.2pp of GDP, but concerns about the
public-debt trajectory persist. The real has depreciated 9% against the
dollar since President Lula’s re-election, prompting the central bank to

intervene in the foreign-exchange market on 19 November.
EMERGING EUROPE
Central Europe
Hungary : Policy rate on hold. As expected, the Central bank main-
tained its policy rate on hold at 5.5% for the 3rd consecutive month.
Whilst inflation is still contained (2% y/y in October), it has been rising
and should pick up further in the coming months due to the end of fuel
price caps and the possible end of food margin caps. Meanwhile, the
future shift in the Central bank’s inflation target to 2.5% ±1% in 2028

and the need to anchor inflation expectations suggest some caution
ahead. In the meantime, Hungary’s 10-year sovereign bond yield has
fallen markedly since last April’s elections (-143 bp). In Poland, cur-
rency and bond markets may be more prone to volatility due to increa-
sing political tensions between the Central bank and the Constitutional
court.
Türkiye
Inflation fears and investment fund scandal shake sentiment. On No-
vember 12, the Turkish central bank raised its end-2027 inflation fo-

recast to 21% from 15%, citing the Iran war. Foreign currency deposits
held by Turkish residents rose $5.2 billion in the week through No-
vember 13 — the biggest weekly increase since July.
COMMODITIES
Oil and gas. Prices fall as markets look through geopolitical risks. Des-
pite a flare-up in attacks in the Strait of Hormuz and Saudi Arabia and
renewed rumours of imminent US-Israeli massive strikes on Iran, Brent
fell 0.5% over the week to $92/bbl as markets focused on the expected

year-end crude surplus forecast by the IEA, and improving refined-pro-
duct inventories. US commercial crude inventories increased another
3.4mb to 418mb, while SPR stocks fell 3.2mb. Diesel cracks declined
10% in the US and 8.5% in Europe. European gas prices rose by 11% to
€94/MWh amid forecasts of cold temperatures for the next two weeks.
y/y = year-on-year
m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels

Document 344: GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし ～2026年9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～

Source PDF: all report pdf/GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし ～2026年9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～.pdf
Report source: DLRI

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026年 11月 9日

1/3 
GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし 
～2026年 9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

1. 2026年 7～9月の収益率はマイナス 1.65％ 
年金積立金管理運用独立行政法人（GPIF）は 2026年 11月6日、同年 7～9月の運用実績を公表
した。期間収益率はマイナス 1.65％だった。7～9月は国内外の長期金利が上昇し、債券価格が下
落した。加えて円高も進み、外国資産の円換算額を押し下げた。資産別の収益率は国内債券がマ
イナス 1.80％、外国債券がマイナス 6.52％、国内株式がプラス 3.71％、外国株式がマイナス
1.86％となった。9月末の運用資産額は314兆3,876億円と 6月末から約 3.4兆円減少した。 
GPIFを巡っては、片山さつき財務・金融相が 7月に「日本の金融資産にさらなる投資をしてい
ただくという方向で後押しをする方策を追求したい」と発言し、ポートフォリオに変化があるか
注目が集まっていた。長期金利が上昇していたこともあり、特に国内債への関心が高かった。 
結論から言うと、国内債比率は基本ポートフォリオの 25％に極めて近く、運用姿勢の変化を読
み取ることは難しい。国内債の比率を引き上げるのではないかという観測は一旦後退するだろ
う。

2. 国内債券比率は 25％近辺を維持  
GPIFの基本ポートフォリオは国内外の株式と債券をそれぞれ 25％とし、国内債券には±6ポイ
ントの乖離許容幅が設定されている。GPIFの公表資料によると、年金積立金全体に占める国内債
比率は 2026年9月末時点で 26.49％だった。これには年金特別会計で管理する積立金、約 5.4兆
円が含まれている。 
この積立金を国内債券と年金積立金全体から除き、GPIFの運用資産ベースで計算すると、9月末
の国内債券比率は25.23％となる。6月末の 24.98％から 0.25ポイント上昇したが、基本ポートフ
ォリオの25％をわずかに上回る水準だ（資料1）。 過去の実績と比較しても、今回の比率は突出し
ていない。2020～2025年度の 9月末平均は 25.05％で、2026年9月末はこれを0.18ポイント上回
る程度だ。

### Page 2

2/3 
資料 1 GPIFの運用資産構成比率（推計、2026年 6月末・9月末）

（注）年金特別会計で管理する積立金を除いたGPIF運用資産ベース。 
（出所）GPIF「2026年度第 1四半期運用状況（速報）」、「同第 2四半期運用状況（速報）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成。

3. 国内債券の買越額は推計 1.4兆円  
次に、7～9月の国内債券への買越額を推計する。6月末と 9月末の国内債券残高の変化から、
期間中の運用損益による増減を除いて推計した。 
年金特別会計で管理する積立金を除いた国内債券残高は、6月末の 79兆3,840億円から 9月末
の79兆3,206億円へ、634億円減少した。一方、7～9月の国内債券の運用損益はマイナス 1兆
4,452億円だった。この差額から、国内債券への買越額は約 1.4兆円と推計される（注 1）。4～6
月の約 5.7兆円と比較すると、国内債券への資金配分額は減少した（注2）。 
同じ方法で他の資産への資金配分を試算すると、外国債券は約 3.3兆円の買い越しとなった。
一方、国内株式は約2.0兆円、外国株式は約 0.8兆円の売り越しとなった。

4. 国内債券の運用姿勢に変化はみられず  
2026年7～9月の運用実績をみる限り、GPIFが国内債券の乖離許容幅を積極的に活用し、基本ポ
ートフォリオの25％を上回る水準へと比率を引き上げようとする動きは確認できなかった。 
国内債券は買い越しているとみられるものの、外国債券には国内債券を上回る資金が配分され
ている。4資産の比率はいずれも25％近い。基本ポートフォリオに近づけて運用する従来の姿勢
が維持されていると考えられる。 
以 上

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
【注釈】
1) 買越額は国内債券残高の変化から運用損益を控除した推計値。国内債券には為替ヘッジ付き外
国債券や円建て短期資産が含まれるため、日本国債の純購入額とは必ずしも一致しない。
2) 2026年7～9月は、GPIFの運用資産額の増減から運用収益額を差し引くと、約 1.9兆円の資金
流入があった計算になる。4～6月はほぼゼロだった。こうした資金流入の違いも、国内債券の
買越額を比較する際には考慮する必要がある。

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢，主要係因美國期中選舉後，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢，從2020年之後，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢，反應其抗通膨保值特性。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化 ， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。

► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業(如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低，有助分散投
資組合對單一AI題材依賴，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療 補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥 價 兩黨均主張降低藥價 製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策 鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾 推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化 有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資
醫療保險覆蓋
支持延長《平價醫療法案》補貼、擴
大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、
醫療服務及保險業長期需求
FDA審查資源 支持增加FDA審查預算與人力配置 著重監管效率優化，額外資源投入 相對有限 兩黨均支持提升新藥審查效率 利多創新藥與生技公司

投資焦點必需消費
► 短期而言，檢視各類股在最近股災表現，美
國成長股、資訊科技、與通訊服務等高波動
度類股，表現明顯落後大盤，其中，資訊科
技與通訊服務波動度最大、跌幅最重，主要
係因美股科技巨頭財報不如預期、負自由現
金流量，疊加期中選舉後民主黨恐緊縮AI基
礎建設進程及資本支出見頂等不確定因素，
令市場 擔憂 導致 資 金流出 ；並轉 進核 心消
費、健康護理、防禦股、公用事業等防禦性
產業，報酬相對較穩健，受市場資金青睞。
► 長期而言，從2008迄今，核心消費類股長期
表現穩健上漲。與主要指數相比，面對近月
AI科技股修正，同期間費半大跌18%、那斯
達克跌幅6%、標普跌幅1%，而核心消費類
股大漲13%，反應其低波動、抗震韌性。
► 整體而言，從2015年至今，歷經2次通膨與
升息循環，核心消費受影響性低，有鑑於資
產配置角度，建議短期跟隨資金流向配置核
心消費股，提高投組抗震韌性。
必需消費長期表現穩健、抗通膨、低波動避險資金流入，必需消費類股單月表現最佳

配置必需消費類股，提高投組抗震韌性

股市
 隨著美國期中選舉結束，美伊衝突可望降溫，市場關注焦點將回歸基本面，惟AI資料中心建設進程
或將受到州政府緊縮政策而造成評價面壓力增添股價波動。短期可布局防禦股、分散配置於不同國
家與產業，增強抗震力、降低市場波動影響，長期而言，AI從全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高
的品質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨勢不變。
 歐、日股可分散配置，利率走升有利銀行股。此外，隨財政政策支持，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高的品
質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨
勢不變，非AI部分可關注必需消費產業、
醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 隨資金湧入，公債殖利率回落，惟根據最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升
息空間，立場偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走升
時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引

力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 聯準會12月份會議升息態度偏鷹，美元高檔整理。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理，日圓弱勢盤整
商品
 美國期中選舉結束，預期中東地緣衝突降溫，油價從近期高點回落，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, declined to –0.14% in October
from +0.06% in September.

The Leading Index weakened again in October after steadier
results through August-September. At –0.14% the latest six
month growth rate is back in negative, below-trend territory,
broadly similar to the soft reads around the start of the year.
The shift points to a return to below-trend growth momentum
in the first half of 2027. Westpac expects annual GDP growth
to slow back to 1.4% for calendar 2026, with a further
moderation to a sluggish 1%yr pace in 2027.

The Leading Index growth rate is still a touch firmer than
six months ago, the current pace of –0.14% comparing to a
–0.2% back in April, when the War in Iran was first impacting.
The component detail shows mixed moves over the
intervening period. Four components have started to exert
a bigger drag: total hours worked taking –0.2ppts off the
headline growth rate; with a further 0.15ppt drag coming
from the combined effect of softer commodity prices

(measured in AUD terms); a narrowing yield spread (mainly
reflecting the RBA tightening pushing up short term rates);
and rising job loss fears captured by the Westpac-Melbourne
Institute Unemployment Expectations Index. This has been
more than offset by a reduced (but still negative) drag from
the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Expectations
Index (adding +0.25ppts); and firmer reads on local equity
markets and dwelling approvals (adding +0.17ppts on a

combined basis.
Overall, the component mix shows labour market conditions
and tightening monetary policy have become more prominent
drags on momentum. While the Leading Index signal is not
overly weak the decline is clearly worth monitoring with the
detail suggesting there may be more weakening to come.
Leading Index moves back below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines

variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken
individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting

Suva
1 Thomson Street
Suva, Fiji
Shamal Chand
Senior Economist

Document 372: Minutes of ECB’s October meeting confirm tightening bias

Source PDF: all report pdf/Minutes of ECB’s October meeting confirm tightening bias.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-26
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

The minutes of the ECB's October meeting conﬁrm a clear
tightening bias and suggest a rate hike at the December
meeting is almost a done deal
Carsten Brzeski
ECB President Christine Lagarde
The just-released minutes of the ECB’s October meeting conﬁrmed
the central bank's current tightening bias. Given that many ECB
members had already been in favour of a rate hike at the October
meeting, a December hike looks almost like a done deal. On a
slightly diﬀerent note, the minutes also shed new light on the ECB’s

internal arithmetic: a large number of members can still fall short of
a majority.
Here are the most important passages from the minutes:
Waiting until December to get a better picture of the longer-term
inﬂation forecast. "The December projections, which would extend
the forecast horizon to include 2029, would provide an updated
integrated assessment of how the energy shock, AI-related

### Page 2

dynamics, the shift in ﬁnancing conditions and all other relevant
developments were shaping the inﬂation outlook and the
surrounding risks."
Concerns about the growth outlook. "Looking ahead, members
generally agreed that tightening ﬁnancial conditions and the
renewed spike in energy prices would have a negative eﬀect on
growth, although its magnitude remained to be seen. The decline in
demand for loans suggested a stronger adverse eﬀect on activity

than had been seen so far, although some of the eﬀect from higher
rates on private investment spending, housing and consumption
might be attenuated by the weaker exchange rate."
Inﬂation remains primarily an energy price shock (for now).
"Members concurred with the assessment presented by Philip Lane
in his introduction, noting that, on the one hand, energy prices had
increased beyond the baseline path foreseen in the September ECB
staﬀ projections, which was likely to raise inﬂation further in the

near term and keep headline inﬂation well above target over the
coming year. On the other hand, underlying inﬂation had remained
broadly stable in recent months and wage growth data had
moderated further."
Some concerns about sticky inﬂation. "Regarding the near-term
outlook, on the one hand, it was clear that inﬂation would be
higher for longer than previously expected. On the other hand, the
latest data for inﬂation in the HICP excluding energy and food

suggested a slightly better outlook than previously expected, but
allowing for pass-through from higher energy prices would suggest
a higher core inﬂation trajectory at or above the level seen in the
adverse scenario. It was suggested that perhaps underlying
inﬂation could become sticky at around 2.5% rather than receding."
Not giving up on those indirect eﬀects. "Regarding the direct,
indirect and second-round eﬀects from higher energy prices, thus

far it was mostly direct eﬀects that were materialising. Indirect
eﬀects had overall remained limited, as expected, and so far there
were no material signs of second-round eﬀects. However, the fact
that energy prices had risen even higher and for longer, together

### Page 3

with the signals from input prices and selling price expectations,
suggested that indirect eﬀects would become stronger over time
and some second-round eﬀects would eventually materialise, as
already anticipated to a degree in the September projections."
A large number is not a majority. "A large number of members
noted that, as the incoming data since the September Governing
Council meeting had conﬁrmed the case for further policy
tightening, they could have supported raising interest rates at the

current meeting. These members stressed that a further rate hike
was warranted in light of the deteriorating inﬂation outlook, while
acknowledging elevated uncertainty, the impact of higher yields on
ﬁnancial conditions and the limited evidence so far of second-
round eﬀects."
All in all, a clear conﬁrmation of the ECB's tightening bias, based on
the assumption that high energy prices will eventually still translate
into broader inﬂationary pressure; a development that is not yet

the case.
Preparing for a December hike
Today's minutes conﬁrmed ECB President Christine Lagarde’s
message at the October press conference. The fact that several ECB
members openly advocated a rate hike, even if they fell short of a
majority, sends a clear signal. If the macro backdrop at the
upcoming December meeting (on 18 December) is similar to the
one in October, a rate hike is a done deal. The only limitations to
this ‘done deal’ scenario we have heard since then, at least from the

doves, are that oil prices and ﬁnancing conditions could move
quickly, potentially in a direction making another hike obsolete.
Looking at these external circumstances, oil prices have indeed
fallen signiﬁcantly since the ECB’s October meeting but are not
much lower than when the September staﬀ projections were
conducted. As regards ﬁnancing conditions, the drop in bond yields
since September will bring little respite for ECB doves. Last but not
least, the latest weakening of the euro will probably enter the ECB’s

radar screen again these days. Since the September projections, the
nominal eﬀective exchange rate has lost some 3%. Adding all these

elements together, there will be very little reason to give the ‘all
clear’ on inﬂation.
Let’s stick with the next round of ECB staﬀ projections for a minute.

Next to changes in the so-called technical assumptions, the most
interesting elements of the forecasts will be the forecasts for wage
growth and core inﬂation. Up to now, the ECB seems to stick to the
traditional approach that higher energy prices will eventually
translate into higher core inﬂation and wage growth. Even if there
haven’t been any indications of such developments so far.
When it comes to wage growth forecasts, in particular, the ECB has

been much more optimistic than, for example, the Survey of
Professional Forecasters. In our view, ﬁnal demand remains under
too much stress from higher energy prices and labour market
insecurity to eventually see a signiﬁcant increase in core inﬂation.
Instead, corporate proﬁt margin squeezing will be the new game in
town. So far, the ECB has had a diﬀerent view. Let’s see whether this
could change at the December meeting.
Today's minutes as well as recent oﬃcial comments conﬁrm our

view that the ECB will hike again by 25bp at the December meeting.
Beyond the December meeting, however, the path for monetary
policy is less obvious. In fact, it will depend on whether the still-not-
visible impact of higher energy prices on the rest of the economy
eventually materialises. We are less convinced than the ECB and
therefore think that additional rate hikes in 2027 would do more
harm than good.

Document 428: US SECTORS CALL_ Information Technology, Communication Services & Financials

Source PDF: all report pdf/US SECTORS CALL_ Information Technology, Communication Services & Financials.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US SECTORS CALL: Information Technology, Communication Services & Financials
Date:    1 hour ago
US SECTORS CALL: Information Technology, Communication Services & Financials

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US SECTORS CALL: Information
Technology, Communication
Services & Financials
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 22 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Steve A Johnson / Unsplash

### Page 2

Last week was brutal. All 11 S&P 500 sectors fell sharply. The S&P 500
dropped 3.7% on Friday alone. The index has fallen for seven days in a
row, its longest losing streak since February 2020. It is up just 1.0% ytd.
The Nasdaq is on its longest weekly losing streak since 2008. The S&P
500 Semiconductors index is down 28.1% mtd.
Last week, we compared November's Friday the 13th S&P 500 close to
August 3, when the index first rose to the same level. We noted that while

the index was relatively calm over that period, there was lots of turbulence
below the surface in the S&P 500 sectors. The calm broke last week. The
S&P 500 is down 9.1% since August 3, and only three sectors remain
above water. Health Care (OW) and Consumer Staples (UW) are up 9.2%
and 8.6% (chart). Energy (OW) is up 2.8% and remains the best sector
performer ytd at a 34.8% gain. Communication Services (MW) is down
19.7%, with Information Technology (MW) and Industrials (OW) down

15.2% and 14.7%.

### Page 3

So far, the selloff has been driven more by increasingly bearish valuation
multiples than by analysts' consensus earnings estimates, which remain
bullish. Let's have a closer look at Information Technology,
Communication Services, and Financials:
(1) Information Technology. Investors don't believe the analysts'
earnings forecasts. Analysts expect IT earnings to grow 58.4% this year
and 48.4% next year. They expect Semiconductors earnings to grow
114.6% and 77.8% this year and next and Semiconductor Equipment

earnings to grow 36.3% and 51.3%. Investors are not paying for that. The
forward P/E of the sector is down 41.9% ytd, while forward earnings is up
71.0% (chart). The stock price index is down 0.7%, after rising over 30%
in early October.

### Page 4

Semiconductors show the gap most clearly. The industry accounts for
54.9% of IT's forward earnings and 38.0% of its market cap. Its forward
P/E has fallen to 9.4, well below the S&P 500's 16.6, and is near its 2010-
11 lows (chart).
Semiconductor Equipment is no different. Its forward earnings is up 89.7%
ytd, while its forward P/E is down 41.8% (chart).
Application Software is the exception. Analysts cut its 2027 earnings
growth forecast to 9.5% from about 17% in August (chart). It is down

28.6% ytd, the worst in the Information Technology industry.

### Page 5

We lowered our sector recommendation to market-weight late last year.
We are sticking with it because we believe the AI buildout will survive CEO
Jensen Huang's resignation from Nvidia.
(2) Communication Services. Meta and Alphabet make up about 40% of
the sector. Both have been caught up in the AI selloff. Meta is down 28%
from its ytd peak, and Alphabet is down 34%. The sector is down 15.5%
ytd, the worst of all 11 S&P 500 sectors. Forward earnings is up 21.5%

ytd, while the forward P/E is down 26.0% (chart).
Analysts expect Interactive Media & Services revenues to grow 25.8% in
2027. Yet they expect earnings to fall 14.2% (chart). Much of that reflects
a base effect from this year's mark-to-market gains posted by Alphabet.

### Page 6

The forward profit margin has also dropped to 26.4% from almost 32%
this summer, partly reflecting the reversal of the mark-to-market impact
next year.
We retain our market-weight rating on Communication Services because a
slower pace of the AI buildout should boost the profitability and cash flow
of the Magnificent-7 including Alphabet and Meta.
(3) Financials. Investors are treating parts of the sector as an AI trade,
which has dragged the whole group down. Investment Banking &

Brokerage fell 11.7% this week, trading down in step with IT. Last week,
we noted how the sector's forward P/E had fallen to 15.2. It is now 14.2,
down from 16.4 at the end of 2025 (chart). Forward earnings is still up
14.1% ytd.

### Page 7

Insurance has been the shelter from the storm. The Property & Casualty
Insurance price index is near a record high, and Insurance Brokers trades
11.8% above its 200-day moving average. Property & Casualty
Insurance's forward profit margin of 13.7% is the highest since 2007
(chart). Analysts expect earnings to grow 11.9% this year but to decline
8.4% next year, so the margin is likely peaking.
Consumer Finance is especially at risk. The industry’s price index is down

21.6% ytd, with a forward P/E that has dropped to 10.2. Yet analysts
expect that industry’s earnings to grow 11.3% this year and 11.4% next
year (chart). The industry is getting cheaper even as earnings grow.
Investors worry that Washington will cap credit card interest rates next
year.

### Page 8

Analysts expect Financials' earnings growth to slow to 7.4% next year
from 16.9% this year. Some de-rating makes sense. But with the sector
trading at 14.2 times forward earnings, we think the market has already
priced in the slowdown. We retain our overweight rating on Financials.

Document 453: 「関西の景気動向」2026年11月号 人手不足とAIの急速な発展のもとで急がれる関西の人材ミスマッチの解消（PDF：1257KB）

Source PDF: all report pdf/「関西の景気動向」2026年11月号　人手不足とAIの急速な発展のもとで急がれる関西の人材ミスマッチの解消（PDF：1257KB）.pdf
Report source: JRI
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

1

2026 年 11 月 
株式会社 日本総合研究所 
調査部 関西経済研究センター 
http://www.jri.co.jp/report/medium/kansai/

１．景気の現状と展望 
 関西の景気は、緩やかな回復基調を維持しつつも、一部に弱い動きがみられる。企業部門では、世界的な
AI 関連需要の拡大が生産や輸出の持ち直しを支えている。設備投資は、⾧引く中東情勢の混乱の影響が懸
念される中でも、企業の収益計画が高めの水準を維持するもとで、拡大基調が続いている。一方、家計部門
では、所得環境の改善が続くものの物価高が重石となり、個人消費の回復ペースは緩慢にとどまっている。 
先行きを展望すると、米中間選挙での与党の敗北を受け、トランプ政権の通商政策における不透明感や
AI・データセンター関連投資を巡る不透明感が強まるようであれば、関西企業の設備投資意欲を減退させる恐
れがある。加えて、中東情勢の混乱を背景とした資源価格の高止まりについても、金利上昇局面における資金
調達コストの増加と相まって、企業収益の重石となる懸念がある。個人消費は、持続的な賃上げの動きが下支
えに寄与するものの、物価高による下押し圧力が根強く、回復ペースは引き続き緩慢にとどまる見通しである。

２．個別指標の動き 
（１） 輸出・生産動向 
関西の輸出額（円ベース）は増加している。国・地域別にみると、中国（含む香港）向けは足元の振れが
大きいものの、アジア（除く中国・香港）向けとともに、半導体等電子部品がけん引し、大幅に増加している。
品目別にみると、半導体等電子部品が含まれる電気機器の増勢が著しい。また、一般機械では半導体等製
造装置が押し上げに寄与している。金属及び同製品も先端材料としての需要増や、それに伴う市況の高騰を
背景に高水準で推移している。なお、WTO の定義に基づき、関西の輸出全体に占める AI 関連財の割合を
算出すると、2024～25 年の平均 23％から、足元で 30％強まで高まっており、世界的な AI 関連需要の拡
大が関西の輸出を押し上げている。

一方、輸出を数量面でみても、緩やかながら回復傾向にある。中国向けや米国向けは、単月の動きの振れ
が大きいが、基調としては底堅く推移している。一方、ASEAN 向けやアジア NIEs 向けは半導体等電子部品
の増加を主因に輸出金額は大きく増加しているが、輸出数量の回復は緩慢にとどまっている。

鉱工業生産は、振れを伴いながらも持ち直している。業種別にみると、世界的な AI 関連需要の拡大を受け
て半導体製造装置の増産が続く生産用機械工業が堅調に推移している。一方、電子部品・デバイス工業は
依然として低水準での推移が続いている。

（２） 企業収益・設備投資動向 
日本銀行大阪支店「短観」（９月調査、全規模・全産業）によると、関西企業全体の 2026 年度の経
常利益計画（全産業・全規模）は前年度比▲7.8％となった。資源価格の高止まりなどによる下押し圧力を
受けて減益見通しとなっているものの、コロナ禍前との比較では、引き続き高めの水準を維持している。

企業が高水準の収益を見込むもとで、関西企業は積極的な設備投資姿勢を維持している。日本銀行大
阪支店「短観」９月調査による 2026 年度の設備投資計画（全産業・全規模）は前年度比＋10.0％と、
過去（2010～2025 年度）の平均伸び率を上回る水準にある。企業規模別にみると、大企業が同＋
8.4％、中堅企業が同＋24.9％、中小企業が同＋2.4％と、いずれも増加する計画となっている。機材やソフ
トウェアの運用・調達コストの上昇などから名目の投資額は押し上げられやすい状況にあるとはいえ、中東情勢
や金利上昇といった懸念材料があるなかでも積極的な投資姿勢に変化はみられない。

（３） 雇用・所得動向 
関西の雇用環境は、人手不足を背景に良好な状態が続いている。雇用関連指標をみると、有効求人倍率
は１倍を超える水準を維持しており、完全失業率（季節調整値）は概ね 2％台後半の低位で推移している。
雇用者数は高めの水準を保っている。 
所得面をみると、府県別「毎月勤労統計調査」から推計した 7～８月の関西の一人当たり現金給与総額
は前年同期比＋4.2％と、高めの伸びを維持している。雇用者報酬を実質ベースでみると、物価高による下押
し圧力が強まるなか、7～8 月は前年同期比＋2.1％と、前期に比べ伸びが鈍化した。消費者物価は、政府
